namespace = guanzhi_court

guanzhi_court.1001 = { # shangshu_fanyanzhijian #1 犯颜直谏
	type = character_event
	title = guanzhi_court.1001.t
	desc = guanzhi_court.1001.desc
	theme = court
	cooldown = { years = 3 }
	left_portrait = { character = root }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:guanzhi_official }

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_vassal = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes } }
			random_vassal = {
				limit = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = guanzhi_official
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1001.o0
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		add_legitimacy = 5
		if = { limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_official } scope:guanzhi_official = { add_opinion = { modifier = guanzhi_opinion_shangshi value = 15 target = root } } }
		random_list = {
			70 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1002 days = 30 } }
			30 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1003 days = 45 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1001.o1
		change_influence = miniscule_influence_value
		random_list = {
			60 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1004 days = 20 } }
			40 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1005 days = 40 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1001.o2
		add_dread = minor_dread_gain
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
		if = { limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_official } scope:guanzhi_official = { add_opinion = { modifier = guanzhi_opinion_caiji value = -20 target = root } } }
		trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1006 days = 60 }
	}

}

guanzhi_court.1002 = { # shangshu_fanyanzhijian #2 君臣相得
	type = character_event
	title = guanzhi_court.1002.t
	desc = guanzhi_court.1002.desc
	theme = court
	left_portrait = { character = root }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:guanzhi_official }

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1002.o0
		change_merit = minor_merit_gain
		scope:guanzhi_official = { change_merit = minor_merit_gain }
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1003 days = 45 }
	}

}

guanzhi_court.1003 = { # shangshu_fanyanzhijian #3 得寸进尺
	type = character_event
	title = guanzhi_court.1003.t
	desc = guanzhi_court.1003.desc
	theme = court
	left_portrait = { character = root }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:guanzhi_official }

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1003.o0
		stress_impact = { base = minor_stress_impact_gain }
		add_legitimacy = 5
		if = { limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_official } scope:guanzhi_official = { add_opinion = { modifier = guanzhi_opinion_shangshi value = 10 target = root } } }
		trigger_event = guanzhi_court.1007
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1003.o1
		scope:guanzhi_official = { guanzhi_revoke_offices_effect = yes }
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
		trigger_event = guanzhi_court.1008
	}

}

guanzhi_court.1011 = { # jiedang_pengdang #1 朋党之迹
	type = character_event
	title = guanzhi_court.1011.t
	desc = guanzhi_court.1011.desc
	theme = court
	cooldown = { years = 3 }
	left_portrait = { character = root }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:guanzhi_official }

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_vassal = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes } }
			random_vassal = {
				limit = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = guanzhi_official
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1011.o0
		add_legitimacy = 5
		add_dread = minor_dread_gain
		random_list = {
			60 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1012 days = 30 } }
			40 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1013 days = 45 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1011.o1
		change_influence = minor_influence_value
		random_list = {
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1014 days = 60 } }
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1015 days = 40 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1011.o2
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
		change_merit = minor_merit_loss
		random_list = {
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1016 days = 90 } }
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1017 days = 120 } }
		}
	}

}

guanzhi_court.1031 = { # shangshu_qingjianfushui #1 旱岁请蠲
	type = character_event
	title = guanzhi_court.1031.t
	desc = guanzhi_court.1031.desc
	theme = court
	cooldown = { years = 3 }
	left_portrait = { character = root }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:guanzhi_official }

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_vassal = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes } }
			random_vassal = {
				limit = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = guanzhi_official
			}
		}
	}

	option = {

	title = guanzhi_court.1163.t
	desc = guanzhi_court.1163.desc
	theme = court
	left_portrait = { character = root }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:guanzhi_official }

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1163.o0
		add_dread = minor_dread_gain
		change_influence = minor_influence_value
		trigger_event = guanzhi_court.1167
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1163.o1
		scope:guanzhi_official = { guanzhi_revoke_offices_effect = yes }
		add_dread = medium_dread_gain
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
		trigger_event = guanzhi_court.1168
	}

}

	}

guanzhi_court.1171 = { # jiedang_tongnianjiedang #1 同年之谊
	type = character_event
	title = guanzhi_court.1171.t
	desc = guanzhi_court.1171.desc
	theme = court
	cooldown = { years = 3 }
	left_portrait = { character = root }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:guanzhi_official }

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_vassal = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes } }
			random_vassal = {
				limit = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = guanzhi_official
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1171.o0
		add_legitimacy = 5
		random_list = {
			60 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1172 days = 30 } }
			40 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1173 days = 45 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1171.o1
		change_influence = miniscule_influence_value
		random_list = {
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1174 days = 25 } }
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1175 days = 25 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1171.o2
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
		random_list = {
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1176 days = 90 } }
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1177 days = 70 } }
		}
	}

}

			limit = { any_vassal = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes } }
			random_vassal = {
				limit = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = guanzhi_official
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1191.o0
		add_legitimacy = 5
		random_list = {
			55 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1192 days = 40 } }
			45 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1193 days = 50 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1191.o1
		change_influence = miniscule_influence_value
		random_list = {
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1194 days = 30 } }
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1195 days = 30 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1191.o2
		change_influence = minor_influence_value
		stress_impact = { base = minor_stress_impact_gain }
		random_list = {
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1196 days = 60 } }
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1197 days = 45 } }
		}
	}

		}

	type = character_event
	title = guanzhi_court.1341.t
	desc = guanzhi_court.1341.desc
	theme = court
	cooldown = { years = 3 }
	left_portrait = { character = root }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:guanzhi_official }

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_vassal = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes } }
			random_vassal = {
				limit = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = guanzhi_official
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1341.o0
		change_merit = minor_merit_gain
		random_list = {
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1342 days = 60 } }
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1343 days = 40 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1341.o1
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
		random_list = {
			60 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1344 days = 60 } }
			40 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1345 days = 40 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1341.o2
		add_treasury = -50
		random_list = {
			60 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1346 days = 90 } }
			40 = { trigger_event = guanzhi_court.1347 }
		}
	}

}

	title = guanzhi_court.1363.t
	desc = guanzhi_court.1363.desc
	theme = court
	left_portrait = { character = root }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:guanzhi_official }

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1363.o0
		change_merit = minor_merit_loss
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
		trigger_event = guanzhi_court.1370
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1363.o1
		add_treasury = -100
		add_dread = minor_dread_gain
		random_list = {
			50 = { trigger_event = guanzhi_court.1368 }
			50 = { trigger_event = guanzhi_court.1369 }
		}
	}

}

guanzhi_court.1371 = { # tingyi_bizhizhizheng #1 钱荒日甚
	type = character_event
	title = guanzhi_court.1371.t
	desc = guanzhi_court.1371.desc
	theme = court
	cooldown = { years = 4 }
	left_portrait = { character = root }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:guanzhi_official }

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_vassal = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes } }
			random_vassal = {
				limit = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = guanzhi_official
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1371.o0
		add_treasury = 50
		random_list = {
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1372 days = 40 } }
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1373 days = 40 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1371.o1
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
		random_list = {
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1374 days = 40 } }
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1375 days = 40 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1371.o2
		change_merit = minor_merit_gain
		random_list = {
			60 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1376 days = 50 } }
			40 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1377 days = 50 } }
		}
	}

}

guanzhi_court.1381 = { # tanwu_tanwuuianfa #1 贪墨案发
	type = character_event
	title = guanzhi_court.1381.t
	desc = guanzhi_court.1381.desc
	theme = court
	cooldown = { years = 3 }
	left_portrait = { character = root }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:guanzhi_official }

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_vassal = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes } }
			random_vassal = {
				limit = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = guanzhi_official
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1381.o0
		add_legitimacy = 10
		add_dread = minor_dread_gain
		random_list = {
			60 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1382 days = 40 } }
			40 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1383 days = 50 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1381.o1
		add_treasury = 100
		add_legitimacy = -5
		random_list = {
			55 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1384 days = 30 } }
			45 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1385 days = 90 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1381.o2
		if = { limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_official } scope:guanzhi_official = { add_opinion = { modifier = guanzhi_opinion_shangshi value = 20 target = root } } }
		change_merit = minor_merit_loss
		random_list = {
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1386 days = 60 } }
			50 = { trigger_event = guanzhi_court.1387 }
		}
	}

}

		random_list = {
			65 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1402 days = 45 } }
			35 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1403 days = 30 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1401.o1
		change_influence = minor_influence_value
		random_list = {
			55 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1404 days = 60 } }
			45 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1405 days = 50 } }
		}

	type = character_event
	title = guanzhi_court.1551.t
	desc = guanzhi_court.1551.desc
	theme = court
	cooldown = { years = 2 }
	left_portrait = { character = root }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:guanzhi_official }

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_vassal = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes } }
			random_vassal = {
				limit = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = guanzhi_official
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1551.o0
		add_legitimacy = 5
		if = { limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_official } scope:guanzhi_official = { add_opinion = { modifier = guanzhi_opinion_shangshi value = 15 target = root } } }
		random_list = {
			60 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1552 days = 30 } }
			40 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1553 days = 45 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1551.o1
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		if = { limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_official } scope:guanzhi_official = { add_opinion = { modifier = guanzhi_opinion_caiji value = -10 target = root } } }
		random_list = {
			55 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1554 days = 50 } }
			45 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1555 days = 35 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1551.o2
		if = { limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_official } scope:guanzhi_official = { add_opinion = { modifier = guanzhi_opinion_shangshi value = 10 target = root } } }
		add_treasury = -50

		}
	}

		name = guanzhi_court.1571.o2

		trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1576 days = 20 }
	}

guanzhi_court.1581 = { # xiangrui_dahanqiyu #1 祈雨不雨
	type = character_event
	title = guanzhi_court.1581.t
	desc = guanzhi_court.1581.desc
	theme = court
	cooldown = { years = 4 }
	left_portrait = { character = root }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:guanzhi_official }

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_vassal = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes } }
			random_vassal = {
				limit = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = guanzhi_official
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1581.o0
		stress_impact = { base = minor_stress_impact_gain }
		add_legitimacy = 5
		random_list = {
			60 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1582 days = 30 } }
			40 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1583 days = 40 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1581.o1
		add_treasury = -100
		random_list = {
			55 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1584 days = 35 } }
			45 = { trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1585 days = 50 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi_court.1581.o2
		add_treasury = -50
		change_merit = minor_merit_gain
		trigger_event = { id = guanzhi_court.1586 days = 45 }
	}

}
